     1→import { Page } from '@playwright/test';
     2→
     3→/**
     4→ * Conflict detection test helpers.
     5→ *
     6→ * Simulates concurrent device publishes by calling the API publish endpoint
     7→ * directly to increment a folder's server-side sequence number, making the
     8→ * web client's local sequence stale.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→/**
    12→ * Bump the server-side sequence number for a folder IPNS record.
    13→ *
    14→ * This simulates another device publishing to the same folder by calling the
    15→ * API publish endpoint without an expectedSequenceNumber (backward-compatible
    16→ * unconditional publish). The server increments the sequence number, making
    17→ * the web client's local sequence stale. The web client's next folder mutation
    18→ * (which sends its now-stale expectedSequenceNumber) will receive a 409.
    19→ *
    20→ * NOTE: Uses a dummy base64 record string which generates a delegated routing
    21→ * warning in API logs because the IPNS record is not cryptographically valid.
    22→ * This is expected -- we only need the DB sequence bump, not a valid IPNS
    23→ * record for DHT propagation. The API still increments the sequence number
    24→ * and returns 200.
    25→ *
    26→ * @param params.page - Playwright page instance (for page.request API calls)
    27→ * @param params.apiBaseUrl - API base URL (e.g., "http://localhost:3000")
    28→ * @param params.accessToken - Bearer token for authenticated API calls
    29→ * @param params.rootIpnsName - IPNS name of the root folder to bump
    30→ * @returns The new server-side sequence number after the bump
    31→ */
    32→export async function bumpServerSequence(params: {
    33→  page: Page;
    34→  apiBaseUrl: string;
    35→  accessToken: string;
    36→  rootIpnsName: string;
    37→}): Promise<{ newSequenceNumber: string }> {
    38→  const { page, apiBaseUrl, accessToken, rootIpnsName } = params;
    39→
    40→  // Step 1: Resolve the current CID from the root folder's IPNS record.
    41→  // The API's DB-cached resolve is reliable and fast (no delegated routing).
    42→  const resolveResponse = await page.request.get(
    43→    `${apiBaseUrl}/ipns/resolve?ipnsName=${encodeURIComponent(rootIpnsName)}`,
    44→    {
    45→      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
    46→    }
    47→  );
    48→
    49→  if (!resolveResponse.ok()) {
    50→    const body = await resolveResponse.text();
    51→    throw new Error(
    52→      `Failed to resolve IPNS for sequence bump (${resolveResponse.status()}): ${body}`
    53→    );
    54→  }
    55→
    56→  const resolved = (await resolveResponse.json()) as {
    57→    success: boolean;
    58→    cid: string;
    59→    sequenceNumber: string;
    60→  };
    61→
    62→  if (!resolved.success || !resolved.cid) {
    63→    throw new Error(
    64→      `IPNS resolve returned success=false or missing CID. Is the vault initialized? Response: ${JSON.stringify(resolved)}`
    65→    );
    66→  }
    67→
    68→  // Step 2: Publish with the same CID but NO expectedSequenceNumber.
    69→  // Omitting expectedSequenceNumber uses the backward-compatible unconditional
    70→  // publish path which always succeeds and increments the sequence by 1.
    71→  //
    72→  // The dummy base64 record string is intentionally invalid as an IPNS record.
    73→  // The API will log a warning when trying to relay it to the delegated routing service,
    74→  // but the DB sequence bump succeeds regardless.
    75→  const dummyRecord = Buffer.from('conflict-test-dummy-record-not-a-valid-ipns-record').toString(
    76→    'base64'
    77→  );
    78→
    79→  const publishResponse = await page.request.post(`${apiBaseUrl}/ipns/publish`, {
    80→    headers: {
    81→      Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
    82→      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    83→    },
    84→    data: {
    85→      ipnsName: rootIpnsName,
    86→      record: dummyRecord,
    87→      metadataCid: resolved.cid,
    88→      // No expectedSequenceNumber -- unconditional publish to simulate another device
    89→    },
    90→  });
    91→
    92→  if (!publishResponse.ok()) {
    93→    const body = await publishResponse.text();
    94→    throw new Error(`Failed to bump server sequence (${publishResponse.status()}): ${body}`);
    95→  }
    96→
    97→  const published = (await publishResponse.json()) as { sequenceNumber: string };
    98→
    99→  return { newSequenceNumber: published.sequenceNumber };
   100→}
   101→
```

> AGENT

Now I understand the full picture. The approach is:

1. **Replace `bumpServerSequence` (direct API call)** with a **second browser session** that performs a real upload through the UI
2. **Remove all `__E2E`** from `main.tsx`
3. **Remove `VITE_E2E`** from the workflow

The second session shares the same wallet account, so it's the same user/vault. When it uploads a file, the server sequence increments naturally, making session A's local sequence stale.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/e2e/utils/conflict-helpers.ts",
  "content": "import { Browser, BrowserContext, Page } from '@playwright/test';\nimport type { PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts';\nimport { setupMockWallet, loginViaWallet } from './wallet-login-helpers';\nimport { createTestTextFile } from './test-files';\nimport { FileListPage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/file-list.page';\nimport { UploadZonePage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/upload-zone.page';\n\n/**\n * Conflict detection test helpers.\n *\n * Simulates concurrent device publishes by using a second browser session\n * logged in as the same user. When the second session uploads a file,\n * the server-side sequence number increments, making the first session's\n * local sequence stale.\n */\n\n/**\n * A second browser session logged in as the same user.\n * Used to simulate another device publishing to the same vault.\n */\nexport interface ConflictDevice {\n  context: BrowserContext;\n  page: Page;\n  fileList: FileListPage;\n  uploadZone: UploadZonePage;\n}\n\n/**\n * Create a second browser session logged in as the same wallet identity.\n *\n * This simulates a second device accessing the same vault. When this\n * session performs mutations (upload, create folder), it bumps the\n * server-side sequence number, making the primary session's local\n * sequence stale.\n *\n * @param browser - Playwright browser instance\n * @param account - Same wallet account as the primary session\n */\nexport async function createConflictDevice(\n  browser: Browser,\n  account: PrivateKeyAccount\n): Promise<ConflictDevice> {\n  const context = await browser.newContext();\n  try {\n    const page = await context.newPage();\n    await setupMockWallet(page, account);\n    await loginViaWallet(page, { timeout: 90_000 });\n\n    // Wait for vault to load\n    await Promise.race([\n      page.locator('.file-list[role=\"grid\"]').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30_000 }),\n      page.locator('[data-testid=\"empty-state\"]').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30_000 }),\n    ]);\n\n    return {\n      context,\n      page,\n      fileList: new FileListPage(page),\n      uploadZone: new UploadZonePage(page),\n    };\n  } catch (err) {\n    await context.close().catch(() => undefined);\n    throw err;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Bump the server-side sequence by uploading a file from the second device.\n *\n * This is a real mutation through the UI — no direct API calls or store\n * access needed. The upload publishes new folder metadata with an\n * incremented sequence number, making any other session's local sequence\n * stale.\n *\n * @param device - Second browser session (conflict device)\n * @param runId - Unique test run identifier for file naming\n * @returns Name of the uploaded bump file\n */\nexport async function bumpSequenceViaSecondDevice(\n  device: ConflictDevice,\n  runId: string\n): Promise<string> {\n  const bumpFileName = `seq-bump-${runId}-${Date.now()}.txt`;\n  const bumpFile = createTestTextFile(bumpFileName, 'sequence bump from second device');\n  await device.uploadZone.uploadFile(bumpFile.path);\n  await device.fileList.waitForItemToAppear(bumpFileName, { timeout: 60_000 });\n  return bumpFileName;\n}\n\n/**\n * Close the conflict device session.\n */\nexport async function closeConflictDevice(device: ConflictDevice): Promise<void> {\n  await device.context.close().catch(() => undefined);\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/e2e/tests/conflict-detection.spec.ts",
  "content": "import { test, expect, Browser, BrowserContext, Page } from '@playwright/test';\nimport type { PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts';\nimport { createTestAccount, setupMockWallet, loginViaWallet } from '../utils/wallet-login-helpers';\nimport { createTestTextFile, cleanupTestFiles } from '../utils/test-files';\nimport {\n  createConflictDevice,\n  bumpSequenceViaSecondDevice,\n  closeConflictDevice,\n  type ConflictDevice,\n} from '../utils/conflict-helpers';\nimport { FileListPage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/file-list.page';\nimport { UploadZonePage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/upload-zone.page';\nimport { CreateFolderDialogPage } from '../page-objects/dialogs/create-folder-dialog.page';\nimport { ContextMenuPage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/context-menu.page';\nimport { ConfirmDialogPage } from '../page-objects/dialogs/confirm-dialog.page';\nimport { TextEditorDialogPage } from '../page-objects/dialogs/text-editor-dialog.page';\n\n/**\n * Conflict Detection E2E Test Suite\n *\n * Verifies the full conflict detection flow end-to-end using two browser\n * sessions logged in as the same user (simulating two devices):\n *\n * 1. Session B uploads a file (bumps server-side sequence)\n * 2. Session A's local sequence is now stale\n * 3. Session A performs a mutation → gets 409 from server\n * 4. Session A auto-resyncs (fetches latest remote state)\n * 5. Session A retries with fresh sequence → operation succeeds\n *\n * Tests cover:\n * - Upload file with stale folder sequence -> auto re-sync -> file visible\n * - Create folder with stale parent sequence -> auto re-sync -> folder visible\n * - Per-file IPNS publish (content update) does NOT trigger conflict detection\n */\ntest.describe.serial('Conflict Detection', () => {\n  let browser: Browser;\n  let context: BrowserContext;\n  let page: Page;\n\n  // Page objects (initialized after login)\n  let fileList: FileListPage;\n  let uploadZone: UploadZonePage;\n  let createFolderDialog: CreateFolderDialogPage;\n  let contextMenu: ContextMenuPage;\n  let confirmDialog: ConfirmDialogPage;\n  let textEditorDialog: TextEditorDialogPage;\n\n  let account: PrivateKeyAccount;\n\n  // Second browser session — same user, simulates another device\n  let deviceB: ConflictDevice;\n\n  // Unique suffix per test run to avoid naming collisions\n  const runId = Date.now().toString();\n\n  // Cleanup: track items created during tests for deletion\n  const createdItems: Array<{ name: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' }> = [];\n\n  test.beforeAll(async ({ browser: testBrowser }) => {\n    test.setTimeout(180_000); // Two Core Kit logins (same identity) + seed upload\n    browser = testBrowser;\n    context = await browser.newContext();\n    page = await context.newPage();\n\n    // Generate a random wallet identity and install mock wallet\n    account = createTestAccount();\n    await setupMockWallet(page, account);\n\n    // Initialize page objects\n    fileList = new FileListPage(page);\n    uploadZone = new UploadZonePage(page);\n    createFolderDialog = new CreateFolderDialogPage(page);\n    contextMenu = new ContextMenuPage(page);\n    confirmDialog = new ConfirmDialogPage(page);\n    textEditorDialog = new TextEditorDialogPage(page);\n\n    // Login via wallet (mock wallet auto-approves connect + SIWE)\n    await loginViaWallet(page, { timeout: 60_000 });\n\n    // Verify we're on the files page and the vault is accessible\n    await page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 60000 });\n    await Promise.race([\n      fileList.fileListContainer().waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30000 }),\n      page.locator('[data-testid=\"empty-state\"]').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30000 }),\n    ]);\n\n    // Seed: upload a file to ensure the root folder IPNS record exists.\n    // For a fresh vault, no IPNS record is published until the first mutation.\n    const seedFileName = `seed-${runId}.txt`;\n    const seedFile = createTestTextFile(seedFileName, 'seed file for conflict tests');\n    await uploadZone.uploadFile(seedFile.path);\n    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(seedFileName, { timeout: 60000 });\n    createdItems.push({ name: seedFileName, type: 'file' });\n\n    // Create second session with the same wallet identity (simulates another device)\n    deviceB = await createConflictDevice(browser, account);\n  });\n\n  test.afterAll(async () => {\n    test.setTimeout(60_000); // Cleanup can be slow with rate-limited API\n\n    // Close second device first\n    if (deviceB) {\n      await closeConflictDevice(deviceB);\n    }\n\n    // Clean up remote items created during tests (runs even if earlier serial tests fail)\n    try {\n      const itemsToDelete = [...createdItems].reverse();\n      for (const item of itemsToDelete) {\n        const isVisible = await fileList.isItemVisible(item.name).catch(() => false);\n        if (!isVisible) continue;\n\n        await fileList.rightClickItem(item.name);\n        await contextMenu.waitForOpen();\n        await contextMenu.clickDelete();\n        await confirmDialog.waitForOpen();\n        await confirmDialog.clickConfirm();\n        await fileList.waitForItemToDisappear(item.name, { timeout: 30000 });\n      }\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Best-effort cleanup -- don't fail the suite if cleanup itself fails\n      console.warn('Cleanup of conflict test items failed:', err);\n    }\n\n    cleanupTestFiles();\n    if (context) {\n      await context.close();\n    }\n  });\n\n  // ============================================================\n  // Test 1: Upload file with stale sequence -> conflict -> retry\n  // ============================================================\n\n  test('upload file to root when sequence is stale -> conflict auto-resolved -> file appears', async () => {\n    test.slow(); // Allow extra time for conflict re-sync cycle\n\n    const fileName = `conflict-upload-${runId}.txt`;\n\n    // Step 1: Bump the server-side sequence by uploading from device B.\n    // This makes device A's local sequence stale.\n    const bumpFile = await bumpSequenceViaSecondDevice(deviceB, runId);\n    createdItems.push({ name: bumpFile, type: 'file' });\n\n    // Step 2: Upload a test file from device A (primary session).\n    // The client will:\n    // - Encrypt the file content and upload to IPFS\n    // - Build the new folder metadata (with the new file added)\n    // - Publish the folder IPNS record with its (now-stale) expectedSequenceNumber\n    // - Receive a 409 Conflict response from the API\n    // - Re-sync: fetch the latest folder metadata from IPNS\n    // - Retry the upload with the fresh sequence number\n    // - Succeed on retry\n    const testFile = createTestTextFile(fileName, `Conflict detection test file - ${runId}`);\n    await uploadZone.uploadFile(testFile.path);\n\n    // Step 3: Assert the file appears in the file list despite the initial\n    // conflict. Use a generous timeout to allow for the re-sync cycle.\n    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(fileName, { timeout: 60000 });\n\n    // Step 4: Assert the operation completed successfully (no persistent error).\n    // The upload zone should not show an error after conflict auto-resolution.\n    const hasUploadError = await uploadZone.hasError();\n    expect(hasUploadError).toBe(false);\n\n    // Track for cleanup\n    createdItems.push({ name: fileName, type: 'file' });\n  });\n\n  // ============================================================\n  // Test 2: Create folder with stale sequence -> conflict -> retry\n  // ============================================================\n\n  test('create folder when parent sequence is stale -> conflict auto-resolved -> folder appears', async () => {\n    test.slow(); // Allow extra time for conflict re-sync cycle\n\n    const folderName = `conflict-folder-${runId}`;\n\n    // Step 1: Bump the server-side sequence again from device B.\n    const bumpFile = await bumpSequenceViaSecondDevice(deviceB, runId);\n    createdItems.push({ name: bumpFile, type: 'file' });\n\n    // Step 2: Create a folder from device A.\n    // The client will hit the same 409 -> re-sync -> retry flow.\n    const newFolderButton = page.locator('.file-browser-new-folder-button');\n    await newFolderButton.click();\n    await createFolderDialog.waitForOpen();\n    await createFolderDialog.createFolder(folderName);\n\n    // Step 3: Assert the folder appears in the file list.\n    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(folderName, { timeout: 60000 });\n\n    // Step 4: Verify no persistent error state after conflict auto-resolution.\n    const folderVisible = await fileList.isItemVisible(folderName);\n    expect(folderVisible).toBe(true);\n\n    // Track for cleanup\n    createdItems.push({ name: folderName, type: 'folder' });\n  });\n\n  // ============================================================\n  // Test 3 (negative): Per-file IPNS publish does NOT conflict\n  // ============================================================\n\n  test('per-file IPNS content update does not trigger conflict even with stale folder sequence', async () => {\n    test.slow(); // Allow for file edit + save cycle\n\n    // Step 1: Upload a text file to edit later.\n    const editFileName = `conflict-edit-target-${runId}.txt`;\n    const originalContent = `Original content for conflict edit test - ${runId}`;\n    const updatedContent = `Updated content via text editor - ${runId} - modified`;\n\n    const testFile = createTestTextFile(editFileName, originalContent);\n    await uploadZone.uploadFile(testFile.path);\n    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(editFileName, { timeout: 30000 });\n\n    // Step 2: Bump the folder's server-side sequence from device B.\n    // Brief pause to avoid API rate limiting from rapid test transitions.\n    await page.waitForTimeout(2000);\n    const bumpFile = await bumpSequenceViaSecondDevice(deviceB, runId);\n    createdItems.push({ name: bumpFile, type: 'file' });\n\n    // Step 3: Open the text editor for the file and save new content.\n    // The text editor save flow publishes only the per-file IPNS record\n    // (NOT the folder IPNS record). Per the Phase 16 design, per-file IPNS\n    // publishes use last-write-wins with no expectedSequenceNumber, so they\n    // never trigger conflict detection.\n    await fileList.rightClickItem(editFileName);\n    await contextMenu.waitForOpen();\n    await contextMenu.clickEdit();\n\n    await textEditorDialog.waitForOpen({ timeout: 15000 });\n    await textEditorDialog.waitForContentLoaded({ timeout: 15000 });\n    await textEditorDialog.setContent(updatedContent);\n    await textEditorDialog.clickSave();\n    await textEditorDialog.waitForClose({ timeout: 30000 });\n\n    // Step 4: Assert the file update completed without a persistent error.\n    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(editFileName, { timeout: 15000 });\n    const fileVisible = await fileList.isItemVisible(editFileName);\n    expect(fileVisible).toBe(true);\n\n    // Step 5: Verify no upload-zone error state.\n    const hasUploadError = await uploadZone.hasError();\n    expect(hasUploadError).toBe(false);\n\n    // Track for cleanup\n    createdItems.push({ name: editFileName, type: 'file' });\n  });\n});\n"
}

Removed `VITE_E2E` env var from CI workflow.
